  let sorted := remaining.mergeSort fun a b => decide (a.fst ≤ b.fst);
  let merged :=
    List.foldl
      (fun acc curr =>
        match acc with
        | [] => [curr]
        | (s, e) :: rest => if curr.fst ≤ e then (s, max e curr.snd) :: rest else curr :: acc)
      [] sorted;
  let cov :=
    List.foldl
      (fun acc x =>
        match x with
        | (s, e) => acc + (e - s))
      0 merged.reverse;
  tmp_lemma_2491.coverage_le_total intervals i
